@dataclass
class Individual:
    species: str = "bear"
    sex: str = "f"               # f, m
    age: str = "adult"           # adult, yearling, young (cub / kitten)
    season: str = "summer"       # spring, summer, autumn, winter
    seed: int = 0
    phase_colour: str = "black"  # bears: black, brown, cinnamon (westside ~all black)
    months: float = 4.0          # age of the young in months (cubs, kittens)

    def spec(self):
        sp = SPECIES[self.species]
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 17)
        j = lambda s: 1 + rng.normal(0, s)
        sp = replace(sp)
        if self.sex == "m" and self.age == "adult":
            if self.species == "bear":
                # boars: bigger, a heavier head and neck, a broader skull
                sp = replace(sp, H=sp.H * 1.18, head_r0=(0.17, 0.17), neck_r0=(0.29, 0.25), head_len=sp.head_len * 1.03)
            else:
                sp = replace(sp, H=sp.H * 1.09, head_r0=(0.14, 0.13), neck_r0=(0.24, 0.14))
        if self.species == "bear" and self.season == "autumn" and self.age != "young":
            # hyperphagia: ~30% heavier by late autumn: the barrel and rump round out
            sp = replace(sp, torso=[(x, zt + 0.015 * (1 - abs(x + 0.2)), zb - 0.03 * max(0, 1 - abs(x + 0.1) / 0.5), hw * 1.08)
                                    for x, zt, zb, hw in sp.torso])
        if self.species == "bear" and self.season == "spring" and self.age != "young":
            sp = replace(sp, torso=[(x, zt - 0.01, zb + 0.03 * max(0, 1 - abs(x + 0.1) / 0.5), hw * 0.92) for x, zt, zb, hw in sp.torso])
        if self.age == "young":
            # cubs and kittens: big head and ears, short body, legs long for the body (kittens),
            # short (cubs); size grows with months
            m = self.months
            if self.species == "bear":
                s = float(np.interp(m, [3, 6, 9, 16], [0.34, 0.45, 0.55, 0.72]))
                sp = replace(sp, torso=[(x * 0.86, zt, 0.4 + (zb - 0.4) * 0.9 + 0.03, hw * 0.9) for x, zt, zb, hw in sp.torso],
                             head_len=sp.head_len * 0.9, head_r0=tuple(v * 1.08 for v in sp.head_r0),
                             head_r1=tuple(v * 0.95 for v in sp.head_r1), muzzle=0.4,
                             ear_len=sp.ear_len * 1.35, ear_w=sp.ear_w * 1.3,
                             neck_len=sp.neck_len * 0.8,
                             fore_root=(sp.fore_root[0] * 0.86, sp.fore_root[1], sp.fore_root[2]),
                             hind_root=(sp.hind_root[0] * 0.86, sp.hind_root[1], sp.hind_root[2]),
                             H=sp.H * s, fur=0.6)
            else:
                s = float(np.interp(m, [2, 4, 8, 14], [0.36, 0.48, 0.66, 0.86]))
                sp = replace(sp, torso=[(x * 0.84, 0.5 + (zt - 0.5) * 0.95, 0.5 + (zb - 0.5) * 0.9 + 0.02, hw * 0.92) for x, zt, zb, hw in sp.torso],
                             head_len=sp.head_len * 1.02, head_r0=tuple(v * 1.12 for v in sp.head_r0),
                             ear_len=sp.ear_len * 1.35, ear_w=sp.ear_w * 1.25,
                             neck_len=sp.neck_len * 0.85, tail_len=sp.tail_len * 0.8,
                             neck_base=(sp.neck_base[0] * 0.84, sp.neck_base[1]),
                             fore_root=(sp.fore_root[0] * 0.84, sp.fore_root[1], sp.fore_root[2]),
                             hind_root=(sp.hind_root[0] * 0.84, sp.hind_root[1], sp.hind_root[2]),
                             fore_r=tuple(v * 1.1 for v in sp.fore_r), hind_r=tuple(v * 1.05 for v in sp.hind_r),
                             H=sp.H * s)
        elif self.age == "yearling":
            sp = replace(sp, H=sp.H * 0.85, torso=[(x, zt, zb + 0.01, hw * 0.9) for x, zt, zb, hw in sp.torso])
        sp = replace(sp, H=sp.H * j(0.03), neck_len=sp.neck_len * j(0.03), head_len=sp.head_len * j(0.02),
                     tail_len=sp.tail_len * j(0.04))
        return sp

    def label(self):
        a = {"adult": {"bear": {"m": "boar", "f": "sow"}, "cougar": {"m": "tom", "f": "female"}},
             "yearling": {"bear": {"m": "yearling", "f": "yearling"}, "cougar": {"m": "subadult", "f": "subadult"}},
             "young": {"bear": {"m": "cub", "f": "cub"}, "cougar": {"m": "kitten", "f": "kitten"}}}
        return f"{self.species} {a[self.age][self.species][self.sex]}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- pose

@dataclass
class Pose:
    body_z: float = 0.0
    pitch: float = 0.0           # nose up +, about `pivot`
    pivot: tuple = (0.0, 0.6)    # (x, z) of the pitch pivot (the hip for rearing, sitting, climbing)
    flex: float = 0.0            # spine flexion: + arches (gathered), - hollows (extended)
    neck_ang: float = None       # None: the species' standing carriage
    head_ang: float = None
    neck_turn: float = 0.0       # yaw of the neck (toward the viewer +)
    head_roll: float = 0.0
    ear_ang: float = 0.0         # ears back (flattened) negative
    tail: tuple = (-70.0, 0.4, 0.0)   # cougar tail: (root angle deg below horizontal -, droop 0..1, lift of the tip)
    tail_swing: float = 0.0      # lateral swing of the tail (deg)
    mouth: float = 0.0
    scap: float = 0.0            # scapulae raised above the back (stalk), in H
    legs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # name -> ("ik", (x, z), meta_deg, digit_deg) or ("fk", (a_up, a_low, a_meta, a_dig))
    lie: float = 0.0
    carry: str = ""              # "salmon": a fish in the mouth
    spread: float = 0.0


LEG_NAMES = ["LF", "RF", "LH", "RH"]


def _std(sp, fore):
    return sp.fore_root if fore else sp.hind_root


def stance_x(sp, fore):
    """Where the ball of the foot sits under a standing animal (x in H)."""
    r = sp.fore_root if fore else sp.hind_root
    if sp.name == "bear":
        return r[0] + (0.05 if fore else 0.12)
    return r[0] + (0.04 if fore else 0.10)


def gait_pose(sp: Species, gait: str, phase: float = 0.0) -> Pose:
    p = Pose()
    bear = sp.name == "bear"
    fx, hx = stance_x(sp, True), stance_x(sp, False)
    FM, HM = sp.fore_meta, sp.hind_meta

    def stand_legs(df=(0.02, -0.03), dh=(-0.02, 0.04)):
        return {"LF": ("ik", (fx + df[0], 0.0), FM, 90), "RF": ("ik", (fx + df[1], 0.0), FM, 90),
                "LH": ("ik", (hx + dh[0], 0.0), HM, 90), "RH": ("ik", (hx + dh[1], 0.0), HM, 90)}

    if gait == "stand":
        p.legs = stand_legs()
        if not bear:
            p.tail = (-78, 0.55, 0.15)
        return p
    if gait == "alert":
        p.legs = stand_legs((0.04, 0.0), (-0.03, 0.02))
        p.neck_ang = sp.neck_ang + (18 if bear else 22)
        p.head_ang = sp.head_ang + 12
        p.ear_ang = 0
        p.tail = (-80, 0.5, 0.3)
        return p
    if gait == "graze":   # bear only: head to the grass, like a cow (the naturalist's "black lump")
        p.legs = stand_legs((0.03, -0.05), (-0.02, 0.05))
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -38.0, -70.0
        p.pitch = -2.0
        return p
    if gait == "sniff":   # bear: nose to the ground while walking slowly
        p.legs = stand_legs((0.06, -0.08), (-0.06, 0.06))
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -22.0, -55.0
        return p
    if gait == "sit":
        if bear:
            # the bear sits on its rump like a person: back up at ~60 deg, hind legs out in front,
            # soles showing, forepaws resting on the belly or the ground between (Seton pl. XVII)
            p.pitch, p.pivot = 58.0, (-0.55, 0.45)
            p.body_z = -0.33
            p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -8.0, -30.0
            p.legs = {"LH": ("fk", (80, 88, 150, 170)), "RH": ("fk", (74, 84, 146, 166)),
                      "LF": ("fk", (-20, -10, 20, 60)), "RF": ("fk", (-28, -12, 18, 55))}
        else:
            # the cat sits: haunches down, hind feet flat under the hips, forelegs straight
            p.pitch, p.pivot = 38.0, (-0.6, 0.55)
            p.body_z = -0.28
            p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = 40.0, -8.0
            p.legs = {"LH": ("fk", (60, -150, 88, 90)), "RH": ("fk", (58, -148, 86, 90)),
                      "LF": ("ik", (0.34, 0.0), 10, 90), "RF": ("ik", (0.29, 0.0), 10, 90)}
            p.tail = (-10, 0.0, 0.0)
            p.tail_swing = 70
        return p
    if gait == "stand_up":   # bear rearing on its hind legs to see and smell (not aggression)
        p.pitch, p.pivot = 74.0, (sp.hind_root[0], sp.hind_root[1])
        p.body_z = 0.18
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -40.0, -60.0
        p.legs = {"LH": ("ik", (sp.hind_root[0] + 0.14, 0.0), 80, 90), "RH": ("ik", (sp.hind_root[0] + 0.02, 0.0), 80, 90),
                  "LF": ("fk", (-60, -40, -30, -20)), "RF": ("fk", (-50, -30, -24, -10))}
        return p
    if gait in ("lie", "sphinx"):
        p.lie = 1.0
        zb = min(st[2] for st in sp.torso)
        p.body_z = -(zb - (0.04 if bear else 0.05))
        if bear:
            # sprawled: forelegs out in front, hind legs trailing, head on the paws or up
            p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -12.0, -18.0
            p.legs = {"LF": ("fk", (80, 88, 92, 92)), "RF": ("fk", (74, 86, 90, 90)),
                      "LH": ("fk", (-80, -86, -100, -120)), "RH": ("fk", (-76, -84, -96, -116))}
        else:
            # sphinx: forelegs straight out in front, hind legs folded beside the body, head up
            p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = 30.0, -10.0
            p.legs = {"LF": ("fk", (-40, 88, 90, 90)), "RF": ("fk", (-44, 86, 90, 90)),
                      "LH": ("fk", (100, -150, 70, 90)), "RH": ("fk", (96, -148, 66, 90))}
            p.tail = (-5, 0.0, 0.0)
            p.tail_swing = 55
        return p
    if gait == "crouch":   # cougar before the pounce: belly to the ground, hind feet gathered under
        zb = min(st[2] for st in sp.torso)
        p.body_z = -(zb - 0.06)
        p.pitch = -4.0
        p.scap = 0.10
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = 4.0, -6.0
        p.legs = {"LF": ("ik", (fx + 0.12, 0.0), 60, 90), "RF": ("ik", (fx + 0.04, 0.0), 60, 90),
                  "LH": ("ik", (hx + 0.12, 0.0), 70, 90), "RH": ("ik", (hx + 0.06, 0.0), 70, 90)}
        p.tail = (-5, 0.05, 0.25)
        p.tail_swing = 0.0
        return p
    if gait == "pounce":
        # the launch and the flight: hind legs driving out behind, forelegs reaching, the body
        # stretched and rising, the tail out straight behind as a balance
        t = phase
        p.flex = -8.0 - 6 * np.sin(np.pi * t)
        p.pitch = 18 * (1 - t) - 10 * t
        p.body_z = 0.05 + 0.55 * np.sin(np.pi * min(t / 0.8, 1.0))
        z = p.body_z
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = 10.0, -8.0
        p.ear_ang = -25
        reach = 0.35 + 0.1 * t
        p.legs = {"LF": ("ik", (fx + reach, max(0.0, z - 0.35 + 0.25 * t)), 60, 120), "RF": ("ik", (fx + reach - 0.06, max(0.0, z - 0.3 + 0.25 * t)), 60, 120),
                  "LH": ("ik", (hx - 0.45, max(0.0, z - 0.72) if t > 0.15 else 0.0), -10, 170), "RH": ("ik", (hx - 0.40, max(0.0, z - 0.7) if t > 0.15 else 0.0), -10, 170)}
        p.tail = (5, 0.0, 0.1)
        return p
    if gait == "climb":
        # bear on a trunk: the body near vertical, forelegs hugging high, hind feet pushing low,
        # moving up in leaps "more like the action of a Cat" (Seton); the trunk face is at x = 0.62
        t = phase
        p.pitch, p.pivot = 82.0, (sp.hind_root[0], sp.hind_root[1])
        p.body_z = 0.35 + 0.12 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * t)
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = 20.0, 0.0
        g = np.sin(2 * np.pi * t)
        tx = 0.1   # trunk front, relative to the hip
        hr = sp.hind_root
        p.legs = {"LF": ("ik", (hr[0] + 0.36, 1.25 + 0.12 * g), 150, 175), "RF": ("ik", (hr[0] + 0.38, 1.12 + 0.12 * g), 150, 175),
                  "LH": ("ik", (hr[0] + 0.30, 0.42 - 0.1 * g), 150, 170), "RH": ("ik", (hr[0] + 0.32, 0.52 - 0.1 * g), 150, 170)}
        p.climb = True
        return p
    if gait == "fish":
        # standing in a riffle, head low over the water watching, weight on the forelegs, one
        # forepaw lifted to pin (phase < 0.5) or the head down with a fish (phase >= 0.5)
        p.legs = stand_legs((0.06, -0.02), (-0.04, 0.05))
        p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -30.0, -62.0
        if phase < 0.5:
            p.legs["LF"] = ("ik", (fx + 0.16, 0.14), 40, 30)
        else:
            p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = -6.0, -30.0
            p.carry = "salmon"
        return p
    if gait == "carry":   # walking with a salmon in the jaws, into the forest
        q = gait_pose(sp, "walk", phase)
        q.neck_ang, q.head_ang = sp.neck_ang + 6, -20
        q.carry = "salmon"
        return q

    # moving gaits: offsets per leg, duty factor, foot excursion (H), lifts, bob
    if bear:
        G = {
            # Shine et al. 2015 (grizzly) and the SICB black-bear abstract: a lateral-sequence walk,
            # a PACE (lateral pairs together) at intermediate speed in black bears, no trot; a
            # canter and gallop at speed. Head low and swinging, the forefoot flips in the swing.
            "walk":   dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "LF": 0.22, "RH": 0.5, "RF": 0.72}, duty=0.64, stride=0.66, lf=0.12, lh=0.10, bob=0.015, neck=4.0, head=-26.0),
            "pace":   dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "LF": 0.08, "RH": 0.5, "RF": 0.58}, duty=0.46, stride=0.80, lf=0.16, lh=0.12, bob=0.03, neck=6.0, head=-22.0),
            "run":    dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "RH": 0.10, "RF": 0.46, "LF": 0.56}, duty=0.32, stride=0.95, lf=0.26, lh=0.20, bob=0.07, neck=4.0, head=-16.0),
            "stalk":  None,
        }[gait if gait in ("walk", "pace", "run") else "walk"]
    else:
        G = {
            # Muybridge pl. 716-720 (cat walking, trotting, galloping), 721-731 (lion, tigress,
            # jaguar walking): a lateral-sequence walk with the hind foot set in the fore print
            # (direct register), a rotary gallop with a huge spine flex, a stalk that is the walk
            # slowed and lowered.
            "walk":   dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "LF": 0.25, "RH": 0.5, "RF": 0.75}, duty=0.66, stride=0.55, lf=0.12, lh=0.12, bob=0.012, neck=12.0, head=-14.0),
            "stalk":  dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "LF": 0.25, "RH": 0.5, "RF": 0.75}, duty=0.76, stride=0.34, lf=0.09, lh=0.08, bob=0.006, neck=-6.0, head=-2.0),
            "trot":   dict(off={"LF": 0.0, "RH": 0.0, "RF": 0.5, "LH": 0.5}, duty=0.42, stride=0.70, lf=0.2, lh=0.16, bob=0.03, neck=16.0, head=-12.0),
            "run":    dict(off={"LH": 0.0, "RH": 0.12, "RF": 0.48, "LF": 0.60}, duty=0.26, stride=1.15, lf=0.30, lh=0.26, bob=0.10, neck=8.0, head=-6.0),
        }[gait]
    p.neck_ang, p.head_ang = G["neck"], G["head"]
    legs = {}
    for name in LEG_NAMES:
        fore = name[1] == "F"
        ph = (phase - G["off"][name]) % 1.0
        ax = fx if fore else hx
        S = G["stride"]
        meta0 = FM if fore else HM
        if ph < G["duty"]:
            s = ph / G["duty"]
            x = ax + S * (0.5 - s)
            z = 0.0
            meta, dig = meta0, 90
            if bear:
                # heel lifts late in stance (the foot peels from the heel)
                meta = meta0 - 40 * max(0, (s - 0.75) / 0.25) ** 1.5
            else:
                meta = meta0 + (8 if fore else 6) * (0.5 - s)
            if gait == "run":
                meta = meta0 * (0.6 if bear else 1.0) + 25 * np.sin(np.pi * s)
        else:
            s = (ph - G["duty"]) / (1 - G["duty"])
            e = 0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * s)
            x = ax + S * (-0.5 + e)
            lift = G["lf"] if fore else G["lh"]
            z = lift * np.sin(np.pi * s ** (0.8 if fore else 1.1))
            sw = np.sin(np.pi * s)
            if bear:
                # the bear's forepaw flips: the wrist flexes and the palm turns up and back in the
                # swing; the hind foot hangs from the ankle, toes down
                meta = meta0 * (1 - sw) + (-70 if fore else 20) * sw
                dig = 90 * (1 - sw) + (-40 if fore else 20) * sw
            else:
                meta = meta0 * (1 - sw) + (-55 if fore else -10) * sw
                dig = 90 * (1 - sw) + (-20 if fore else 30) * sw
        legs[name] = ("ik", (x, z), meta, dig)
    p.legs = legs
    if gait in ("walk", "stalk", "pace"):
        p.body_z = -G["bob"] * np.cos(4 * np.pi * phase)
        p.head_ang += (6 if not bear else 4) * np.sin(4 * np.pi * phase)
        if bear:
            p.neck_turn = 6 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * phase)        # the low head swings side to side
        else:
            p.tail = (-80, 0.6, 0.2)
            p.tail_swing = 12 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * phase)
    if gait == "stalk":
        # belly low, legs flexed, head below the shoulder line, the scapulae standing up
        zb = min(st[2] for st in sp.torso)
        p.body_z += -0.20
        p.scap = 0.08
        p.tail = (-35, 0.3, 0.12)
    if gait == "trot":
        p.body_z = G["bob"] * np.cos(4 * np.pi * phase)
    if gait == "run":
        # rotary gallop: gathered (spine arched, all four under) ~0.9, extended ~0.4
        p.body_z = G["bob"] * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2 * np.pi * (phase - 0.9)))
        p.flex = (16 if not bear else 10) * np.cos(2 * np.pi * (phase - 0.9))
        p.pitch = 5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (phase - 0.25))
        p.neck_ang += 6 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (phase - 0.1))
        p.tail = (-15, 0.1, 0.25)
        p.ear_ang = -15
    return p


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- geometry helpers

def _dir(ang_deg, turn_deg=0.0):
    t, u = np.radians(ang_deg), np.radians(turn_deg)
    return np.array([np.cos(t) * np.cos(u), np.cos(t) * np.sin(u), np.sin(t)])


def ik2(root, target, L1, L2, bend_forward):
    root = np.asarray(root, float)
    target = np.asarray(target, float)
    d = target - root
    D = np.linalg.norm(d)
    D = np.clip(D, abs(L1 - L2) + 1e-4, L1 + L2 - 1e-4)
    d = d / (np.linalg.norm(d) + 1e-9)
    a = (L1 * L1 - L2 * L2 + D * D) / (2 * D)
    h = np.sqrt(max(L1 * L1 - a * a, 0.0))
    perp = np.array([-d[1], d[0]])
    if (perp[0] > 0) != bend_forward:
        perp = -perp
    return root + d * a + perp * h


def loft(stations, n=24):
    st = np.array(stations, float)
    st = st[np.argsort(st[:, 0])]
    xx = np.linspace(st[0, 0], st[-1, 0], n)
    from scipy.interpolate import PchipInterpolator
    out = [xx] + [PchipInterpolator(st[:, 0], st[:, k])(xx) for k in range(1, 4)]
    return np.stack(out, 1)


def tube_from_points(name, pts, ra, rb, up, mat, thin=False, n=None, cap0=True, cap1=True):
    pts = np.asarray(pts, float)
    ra = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(ra, float), (len(pts),)).copy()
    rb = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(rb, float), (len(pts),)).copy()
    if n is not None and n != len(pts):
        seg = np.linalg.norm(np.diff(pts, axis=0), axis=1)
        s = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(seg)])
        t = np.linspace(0, s[-1], n)
        pts = np.stack([np.interp(t, s, pts[:, k]) for k in range(3)], 1)
        ra = np.interp(t, s, ra)
        rb = np.interp(t, s, rb)
    return Tube(name, pts, ra, rb, np.asarray(up, float), mat, thin, cap0, cap1)


def _vec(ang):
    """Unit vector in (x, z) for an angle from straight down, forward positive."""
    a = np.radians(ang)
    return np.array([np.sin(a), -np.cos(a)])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- build

def build(ind: Individual, pose: Pose):
    sp = ind.spec()
    tubes = []
    H = sp.H
    bear = sp.name == "bear"
    neck_ang = sp.neck_ang if pose.neck_ang is None else pose.neck_ang
    head_ang = sp.head_ang if pose.head_ang is None else pose.head_ang
    pv = np.array([pose.pivot[0], 0.0, pose.pivot[1]])
    fpiv = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 0.75])

    def body_xf(p):
        p = np.asarray(p, float)
        out = p.copy()
        if pose.flex:
            w = np.clip(p[..., 0] / 0.5, -1, 1)
            t = np.radians(-pose.flex * w)
            dx = out[..., 0] - fpiv[0]
            dz = out[..., 2] - fpiv[2]
            out[..., 0] = fpiv[0] + np.cos(t) * dx - np.sin(t) * dz
            out[..., 2] = fpiv[2] + np.sin(t) * dx + np.cos(t) * dz
        if pose.pitch:
            t = np.radians(pose.pitch)
            dx = out[..., 0] - pv[0]
            dz = out[..., 2] - pv[2]
            out[..., 0] = pv[0] + np.cos(t) * dx - np.sin(t) * dz
            out[..., 2] = pv[2] + np.sin(t) * dx + np.cos(t) * dz
        out[..., 2] += pose.body_z
        return out

    # --- torso
    L = loft(sp.torso, 30)
    x, zt, zb, hw = L.T
    zc = (zt + zb) / 2
    c = body_xf(np.stack([x, np.zeros_like(x), zc], 1))
    spread = 1.0 + (0.22 if bear else 0.12) * pose.lie
    torso = Tube("torso", c, (zt - zb) / 2, hw * spread, np.array([0, 0, 1.0]), "torso")
    torso.xs, torso.zc, torso.ra, torso.rb = x, zc, (zt - zb) / 2, hw
    tubes.append(torso)

    # --- scapulae: the cougar's shoulder blades stand up above the back when it stalks
    for side in (1, -1):
        st = np.array([sp.scap_top[0], side * sp.fore_root[2] * 0.8, sp.scap_top[1] - 0.06 + pose.scap])
        sj = np.array([sp.fore_root[0] + 0.02, side * (sp.fore_root[2] + 0.02), sp.fore_root[1]])
        pts = body_xf(np.array([st, st * 0.5 + sj * 0.5, sj]))
        if bear:
            ra, rb = [0.10, 0.15, 0.14], [0.07, 0.10, 0.10]
        else:
            ra, rb = [0.06, 0.10, 0.085], [0.04, 0.07, 0.07]
        tubes.append(tube_from_points(f"shoulder{side}", pts, ra, rb, [1, 0, 0], "shoulder", n=8))

    # --- neck
    nb = body_xf(np.array([sp.neck_base[0], 0.0, sp.neck_base[1]]))
    ndir = _dir(neck_ang + pose.pitch, pose.neck_turn)
    poll = nb + ndir * sp.neck_len
    npts = np.array([nb - ndir * 0.12, nb, nb + ndir * sp.neck_len * 0.4, nb + ndir * sp.neck_len * 0.75, poll])
    r0a, r0b = sp.neck_r0
    r1a, r1b = sp.neck_r1
    na = np.array([r0a * 1.05, r0a, r0a * 0.6 + r1a * 0.4, r0a * 0.3 + r1a * 0.7, r1a])
    nbw = np.array([r0b * 1.05, r0b, r0b * 0.65 + r1b * 0.35, r0b * 0.35 + r1b * 0.65, r1b])
    nup = np.cross(np.cross(ndir, [0, 0, 1.0]), ndir)
    if np.linalg.norm(nup) < 1e-6:
        nup = np.array([-1.0, 0, 0])
    nup /= np.linalg.norm(nup)
    tubes.append(tube_from_points("neck", npts, na, nbw, nup, "neck", n=16))

    # --- head
    hd = _dir(head_ang + pose.pitch, pose.neck_turn * 1.4)
    hup = np.cross(np.cross(hd, [0, 0, 1.0]), hd)
    if np.linalg.norm(hup) < 1e-6:
        hup = np.array([-1.0, 0, 0])
    hup /= np.linalg.norm(hup)
    h0 = poll + ndir * 0.02
    hl = sp.head_len
    mz = sp.muzzle
    ca, cb = sp.head_r0
    ma, mb = sp.head_r1
    if bear:
        # the black bear's face: a broad skull, then a long straight muzzle tapering to the nose,
        # no dish and no stop to speak of (a grizzly's face is dished); the profile is a line
        u = np.array([-0.05, 0.12, 0.32, 1 - mz, 1 - mz * 0.55, 0.9, 1.0])
        ra = np.array([ca * 0.85, ca, ca * 0.95, ca * 0.62, ma * 1.18, ma * 1.0, ma * 0.8])
        rbb = np.array([cb * 0.85, cb, cb * 0.98, cb * 0.6, mb * 1.12, mb * 1.0, mb * 0.8])
        drop = np.array([0, 0, 0.0, 0.25, 0.45, 0.55, 0.6]) * ca * 0.5
    else:
        # the cat's head: a round skull, a short broad muzzle below the eyes, a rounded chin
        u = np.array([-0.08, 0.15, 0.40, 1 - mz, 1 - mz * 0.45, 0.92, 1.0])
        ra = np.array([ca * 0.8, ca, ca * 0.96, ca * 0.72, ma * 1.1, ma, ma * 0.78])
        rbb = np.array([cb * 0.8, cb, cb * 1.0, cb * 0.82, mb * 1.08, mb, mb * 0.75])
        drop = np.array([0, 0, 0.05, 0.4, 0.7, 0.78, 0.8]) * ca * 0.55
    hpts = h0[None] + hd[None] * (u[:, None] * hl) - hup[None] * drop[:, None]
    head = tube_from_points("head", hpts, ra, rbb, hup, "head", n=14)
    head.axis = hd
    tubes.append(head)
    if not bear:
        # the cat's cheeks and jowls widen the lower face: a short cross-tube under the eyes
        jc = h0 + hd * hl * 0.5 - hup * ca * 0.45
        tubes.append(tube_from_points("jaw", np.array([jc - hd * 0.05, jc + hd * 0.08]), [ca * 0.5, ma * 0.8], [cb * 0.95, mb * 1.05], hup, "head", n=5))
    if pose.mouth > 0:
        jd = _dir(head_ang + pose.pitch - 28 * pose.mouth, pose.neck_turn * 1.4)
        j0 = h0 + hd * hl * 0.45 - hup * ca * 0.5
        tubes.append(tube_from_points("jawopen", np.array([j0, j0 + jd * hl * 0.5]), [ma * 0.7, ma * 0.5], [mb * 0.8, mb * 0.6], hup, "head", n=6))

    # --- ears: rounded, standing up from the top-back of the skull
    for side in (1, -1):
        base = h0 + hd * hl * 0.12 + hup * ca * 0.82 + np.array([0, side * cb * (0.62 if bear else 0.55), 0])
        ea = np.radians(pose.ear_ang)
        out = np.array([0, side, 0.0])
        d = hup * np.cos(ea) - hd * np.sin(-ea) * 1.0 + out * (0.35 if bear else 0.25) - hd * 0.1
        d /= np.linalg.norm(d)
        el = sp.ear_len
        epts = np.array([base, base + d * el * 0.5, base + d * el])
        en = np.cross(d, out) if abs(d @ out) < 0.95 else hd
        en /= np.linalg.norm(en) + 1e-9
        tubes.append(tube_from_points(f"ear{side}", epts, [sp.ear_w * 0.25, sp.ear_w * 0.22, sp.ear_w * 0.12],
                                      [sp.ear_w * 0.55, sp.ear_w * 0.6, sp.ear_w * 0.3], en, "ear", thin=True, n=6))

    # --- carried salmon
    if pose.carry == "salmon":
        mcen = h0 + hd * hl * 0.8 - hup * ca * 0.4
        lat = np.array([0, 1.0, 0])
        fl = 0.55 if bear else 0.4
        fpts = np.array([mcen + lat * fl * 0.45 - hup * 0.05, mcen, mcen - lat * fl * 0.5 - hup * 0.12])
        tubes.append(tube_from_points("salmon", fpts, [0.035, 0.06, 0.03], [0.02, 0.03, 0.015], [0, 0, 1.0], "salmon", n=8))

    # --- tail
    tr = body_xf(np.array([sp.torso[-1][0] + 0.06, 0.0, sp.torso[-1][1] + (0.10 if not bear else 0.12)]))
    if bear:
        td = _dir(-150 + pose.pitch, 0)
        tpts = np.array([tr, tr + np.array([-1, 0, 0]) * 0.04 + np.array([0, 0, -1]) * sp.tail_len])
        tubes.append(tube_from_points("tail", tpts, [sp.tail_r, sp.tail_r * 0.7], [sp.tail_r * 1.2, sp.tail_r * 0.8], [1, 0, 0], "tail", n=4))
    else:
        root_ang, droop, lift = pose.tail
        n = 14
        Lt = sp.tail_len
        pts = [tr]
        a = root_ang + pose.pitch * 0.5
        sw = np.radians(pose.tail_swing)
        d = tr.copy()
        for i in range(1, n):
            u = i / (n - 1)
            # the tail leaves the croup, falls, then its last third curls up: the J
            ang = a - 60 * droop * u + (120 * lift + 30 * droop) * max(0, (u - 0.62) / 0.38) ** 1.5
            step = np.array([-np.cos(np.radians(ang)) * np.cos(sw * u), np.cos(np.radians(ang)) * np.sin(sw * u) * 1.0, np.sin(np.radians(ang))])
            d = d + step * Lt / (n - 1)
            if d[2] < 0.02 and pose.lie < 0.5:
                d[2] = 0.02
            pts.append(d.copy())
        pts = np.array(pts)
        pts[:, 2] = np.maximum(pts[:, 2], 0.02)
        r = sp.tail_r
        ra = np.linspace(r * 1.25, r * 0.95, n)
        ra[-3:] *= [1.05, 1.1, 0.9]      # the tip is bushy
        tubes.append(tube_from_points("tail", pts, ra, ra, [0, 0, 1.0], "tail", n=18))
        tubes[-1].thin = True

    # --- legs
    for name in LEG_NAMES:
        tubes += leg(sp, pose, name, body_xf)

    for t in tubes:
        t.c = t.c * H
        t.a = t.a * H
        t.b = t.b * H
    return tubes, sp


def leg(sp, pose, name, body_xf):
    fore = name[1] == "F"
    bear = sp.name == "bear"
    side = 1 if name[0] == "L" else -1
    rx0, rz0, ry0 = sp.fore_root if fore else sp.hind_root
    root = body_xf(np.array([rx0, side * ry0, rz0]))
    Lu, Ll, Lm, Ld = sp.fore_len if fore else sp.hind_len
    ru, rl, rm, rp = sp.fore_r if fore else sp.hind_r
    kind, *args = pose.legs[name]
    R = np.array([root[0], root[2]])
    if kind == "ik":
        (fx, fz), meta, dig = args
        ball = np.array([fx, max(fz, 0.0) + rp * 0.55])
        wrist = ball - _vec(meta) * Lm
        joint = ik2(R, wrist, Lu, Ll, bend_forward=not fore)
        toe = ball + _vec(dig) * Ld
    else:
        a1, a2, a3, a4 = args[0]
        joint = R + _vec(a1) * Lu
        wrist = joint + _vec(a2) * Ll
        ball = wrist + _vec(a3) * Lm
        toe = ball + _vec(a4) * Ld
    y = root[1]
    P = [np.array([p[0], y, p[1]]) for p in (R, joint, wrist, ball, toe)]
    toe_in = np.radians(sp.toe_in if fore else sp.toe_in * 0.3)
    out = []
    # upper segment: the arm / thigh mass, running up into the body
    if fore:
        top = P[0] + np.array([0.0, 0, 0.08])
        seg1 = np.array([top, P[0], P[0] * 0.5 + P[1] * 0.5, P[1]])
        ra1 = np.array([ru * 1.1, ru, ru * 0.9, rl * 1.15])
    else:
        # the ham: from under the croup, its rear edge continuous with the buttock; the cougar's
        # thigh is the biggest muscle mass on the animal
        top = P[0] + np.array([-0.06, 0, 0.10])
        seg1 = np.array([top, P[0], P[0] * 0.45 + P[1] * 0.55, P[1]])
        ra1 = np.array([ru * 1.2, ru * 1.1, ru * 0.9, rl * 1.2])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "1", seg1, ra1, ra1 * (0.75 if bear else 0.62), [1, 0, 0], "leg1", n=10))
    # lower segment: forearm / shank; the bear's are hairy columns, the cat's taper to the wrist
    seg2 = np.array([P[1], P[1] * 0.5 + P[2] * 0.5, P[2]])
    ra2 = np.array([rl * 1.1, rl, rm * (1.2 if bear else 1.1)])
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "2", seg2, ra2, ra2 * (0.85 if bear else 0.72), [1, 0, 0], "leg2", n=8, thin=not bear))
    # metapodial: the bear's flat foot / palm, the cat's sloping metapodial
    seg3 = np.array([P[2], P[3]])
    if not fore and not bear:
        # the hock point sticks out behind (the calcaneus)
        dd = P[2] - P[1]
        dd /= np.linalg.norm(dd) + 1e-9
        hp = P[2] + np.array([-1.0, 0, 0]) * rm * 1.1 + np.array([0, 0, rm * 0.5])
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "hk", np.array([P[2] - dd * rm * 2.0, hp]), [rm * 0.9, rm * 0.6], [rm * 0.6, rm * 0.45], [0, 0, 1], "leg", thin=True, n=4))
    if bear:
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "3", seg3, [rm, rm * 0.95], [rm * 1.1, rp * 1.1], [0, 0, 1], "paw", n=5))
    else:
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "3", seg3, [rm, rm * 0.9], [rm * 0.9, rm * 0.9], [1, 0, 0], "leg", n=5, thin=True))
    # the paw: a round pad of toes; the bear's turned in
    d4 = P[4] - P[3]
    L4 = np.linalg.norm(d4) + 1e-9
    d4n = d4 / L4
    if fore and toe_in:
        d4n = np.array([d4n[0] * np.cos(toe_in), -side * np.sin(toe_in) * abs(d4n[0]), d4n[2]])
        d4n /= np.linalg.norm(d4n)
    pw0 = P[3] - d4n * rp * 0.2
    pw1 = P[3] + d4n * L4
    out.append(tube_from_points(name + "pw", np.array([pw0, pw0 * 0.5 + pw1 * 0.5, pw1]), [rp * 0.7, rp * 0.62, rp * 0.4], [rp * 0.95, rp, rp * 0.7],
                                [0, 0, 1], "paw", n=5))
    if bear:
        # claws: short, curved, dark (the black bear's front claws are short and curved)
        cl0 = pw1
        out.append(tube_from_points(name + "cl", np.array([cl0, cl0 + d4n * rp * 0.5 + np.array([0, 0, -rp * 0.25])]), [rp * 0.12, rp * 0.05], [rp * 0.5, rp * 0.3], [0, 0, 1], "claw", thin=True, n=3))
    for t in out:
        t.leg = name
    return out


def hb_length(ind):
    """Nose to tail root in metres, standing side view (the size convention: size_px is this)."""
    sp = ind.spec()
    tubes, _ = build(ind, gait_pose(sp, "stand"))
    xs = np.concatenate([t.c[:, 0] for t in tubes if t.name in ("torso", "head", "neck")])
    tor = [t for t in tubes if t.name == "torso"][0]
    return xs.max() - (tor.c[:, 0].min() - tor.b[0] * 0)

Write: elements/bear-cougar/src/coat.py
"""Coat regions: which material each surface point wears.

Black bear (westside, black phase): glossy black all over; a brown-to-tan muzzle (the "brown
nose" of the black bear, the only warm patch on the animal and the one that says which way the
head points); a white chest blaze on some individuals (a V or a crescent on the brisket); the
insides of the ears a shade lighter; short dark claws. Brown and cinnamon phases: the same pattern
in brown, with the legs darker than the body and the muzzle paler. [Seton 1926; naturalist
predators.md]

Cougar: uniform tawny, "a pale yellowish sandy brown" (westside, Felis concolor olympus, "very
dark in colour"); pale cream belly, inner legs, chin, throat and upper lip; a black spot at the
base of the whiskers; the backs of the ears dark with a lighter centre; the tail tip nearly black;
a darker saddle down the spine. Kittens: yellowish brown with black spots and a ringed tail till
about 6 months. [Seton 1925, 1896]
"""
import numpy as np
from rig import (BODY, DARK, PALE, BELLY, LEG, CLAW, SPOT, TIP, NOSE, EARIN, THROAT, TAILTIP,
                 MUZZLE, BLAZE, EYE, FACE)

NMAT = 16
PRIORITY = [NOSE, EYE, TAILTIP, SPOT, MUZZLE]


def _hash(a, b, seed):
    h = (np.floor(a).astype(np.int64) * 73856093) ^ (np.floor(b).astype(np.int64) * 19349663) ^ (seed * 83492791)
    return ((h % 1000003) / 1000003.0 + 1) % 1.0


def kitten_spots(x, cth, sth, seed, xr, xf):
    """Kitten spots: irregular dark blotches in loose rows along the flank, bigger on the back."""
    ang = np.arctan2(sth, cth)
    out = np.zeros(len(x), bool)
    for k, (cl, off, dx, r) in enumerate(((0.8, 0.0, 0.085, 0.024), (0.45, 0.5, 0.075, 0.021), (0.1, 0.2, 0.07, 0.018), (-0.3, 0.7, 0.065, 0.015))):
        a0 = np.arccos(np.clip(cl, -1, 1))
        for sgn in (1, -1):
            along = (x - xr) / dx + off
            i = np.round(along)
            jit = (_hash(i, k * 7 + (sgn > 0), seed) - 0.5) * 0.6
            d_al = (along - i - jit) * dx
            d_ac = (np.abs(ang) - a0) * 0.16
            rr = r * (0.7 + 0.6 * _hash(i, k * 11 + (sgn > 0), seed + 2))
            hit = (d_al ** 2 + (d_ac * 1.3) ** 2 < rr * rr) & (np.sign(ang + 1e-9) == sgn) & (x > xr + 0.05) & (x < xf - 0.06)
            out |= hit
    return out


def has_blaze(ind):
    return ind.species == "bear" and ind.phase_colour == "black" and _hash(np.array([ind.seed]), np.array([3]), 1)[0] < 0.35


def materials(ind, tb, p, n, u, cth, sth):
    sp = ind.spec()
    nm = tb.name
    bear = ind.species == "bear"
    young = ind.age == "young"
    kit_spots = (not bear) and young and ind.months < 7
    out = np.full(len(p), BODY, np.int32)
    if nm == "torso":
        uu = np.linspace(0, 1, len(tb.xs))
        x = np.interp(u, uu, tb.xs)
        xr, xf = sp.torso[0][0] if sp.torso[0][0] < sp.torso[-1][0] else sp.torso[-1][0], max(sp.torso[0][0], sp.torso[-1][0])
        if bear:
            out[:] = BODY
            if has_blaze(ind):
                # the chest blaze: a pale V on the brisket, low on the front of the chest
                out[(x > xf - 0.1) & (cth < 0.1) & (cth > -0.9) & (np.abs(sth) < 0.55)] = BLAZE
            if ind.phase_colour != "black":
                out[cth < -0.55] = DARK
        else:
            out[:] = BODY
            out[cth < -0.45] = BELLY                          # the pale underside
            out[(cth > 0.9)] = DARK                           # the darker saddle down the spine
            out[(x > xf - 0.12) & (cth < 0.0)] = PALE         # pale chest and throat base
            if kit_spots:
                out[kitten_spots(x, cth, sth, ind.seed, xr, xf) & (cth > -0.5)] = SPOT
    elif nm.startswith("shoulder"):
        out[:] = BODY
        if not bear and kit_spots:
            out[_hash(u * 9, cth * 4, ind.seed) < 0.18] = SPOT
    elif nm == "neck":
        out[:] = BODY
        if not bear:
            out[cth < -0.35] = PALE                           # cream throat
            if kit_spots:
                out[(cth > 0.2) & (_hash(u * 8, sth * 5, ind.seed) < 0.25)] = SPOT
        elif ind.phase_colour != "black":
            out[:] = BODY
    elif nm in ("head", "jaw", "jawopen"):
        if bear:
            out[:] = BODY
            # the brown muzzle: the front half of the head, all round, up to just below the eyes
            if nm == "head":
                out[(u > 0.5) & ~((cth > 0.55) & (u < 0.62))] = MUZZLE
                out[u > 0.95] = NOSE
                eye = (np.abs(u - 0.42) < 0.05) & (cth > 0.1) & (cth < 0.6) & (np.abs(sth) > 0.75)
                out[eye] = EYE
            else:
                out[:] = MUZZLE
        else:
            out[:] = BODY
            if nm == "head":
                out[(u > 0.35) & (u < 0.62) & (cth > 0.5)] = FACE      # the darker bridge of the nose
                out[(u > 0.6) & (cth < 0.35)] = PALE                    # upper lip and chin cream
                out[(u > 0.62) & (u < 0.8) & (cth > -0.1) & (cth < 0.4) & (np.abs(sth) > 0.8)] = DARK   # whisker patch
                out[u > 0.95] = NOSE
                eye = (np.abs(u - 0.43) < 0.06) & (cth > 0.2) & (cth < 0.75) & (np.abs(sth) > 0.6)
                out[eye] = EYE
            else:
                out[:] = PALE
    elif nm.startswith("ear"):
        out[:] = DARK if not bear else BODY
        inner = cth > 0
        out[inner & (u < 0.8)] = EARIN
    elif nm == "tail":
        if bear:
            out[:] = BODY
        else:
            out[:] = BODY
            out[cth < -0.4] = BELLY
            out[u > 0.86] = TAILTIP
            if young and ind.months < 8:
                out[(np.floor(u * 11) % 2 == 1) & (u > 0.35)] = TAILTIP   # the ringed kitten tail
    elif nm[:2] in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH"):
        seg = nm[2:]
        if bear:
            out[:] = LEG if ind.phase_colour != "black" else BODY
            if seg == "cl":
                out[:] = CLAW
        else:
            out[:] = BODY
            if seg in ("2", "3"):
                out[cth < -0.2] = BELLY if seg == "2" else BODY
            if seg == "1":
                out[(u > 0.5) & (n[:, 1] * (1 if nm[0] == "L" else -1) < -0.3)] = BELLY   # inner thigh
            if seg == "pw":
                out[:] = BODY
            if kit_spots and seg in ("1", "2"):
                out[_hash(u * 7, cth * 3 + (nm[0] == "L"), ind.seed + len(nm)) < 0.2] = SPOT
    elif nm == "salmon":
        out[:] = TIP
        out[cth > 0.3] = THROAT
    return out


def axis_materials(ind, tb, u):
    n = len(u)
    cth = np.zeros(n) + 0.6
    sth = np.ones(n)
    return materials(ind, tb, tb.c, np.zeros((n, 3)), u, cth, sth)

def coat_table(ind):
    if ind.species == "bear":
        t = dict({"black": BEAR_BLACK, "brown": BEAR_BROWN, "cinnamon": BEAR_CINNAMON}[ind.phase_colour])
        if ind.season == "spring" and ind.age != "young":
            # a bear out of the den: the coat duller and browner at the tips (sun-bleached, worn)
            d, l = t[rig.BODY]
            t[rig.BODY] = (d, mix(l, (120, 96, 80), 0.25))
    else:
        t = dict(COUGAR)
        if ind.age == "young" and ind.months < 7:
            t.update(KITTEN)
        if ind.season == "winter" and ind.age != "young":
            for m in (rig.BODY, rig.BELLY):
                d, l = t[m]
                t[m] = (desat(d, 0.75, 1.0), desat(l, 0.75, 1.02))
    for m in range(16):
        if m not in t:
            t[m] = t[rig.BODY]
    return t


def desat(c, s, lk=1.0):
    lab = to_lab(c)
    lab[1:] *= s
    lab[0] *= lk
    return to_rgb(lab)

Write: elements/bear-cougar/src/paint.py
"""The painter: an Individual + gait/pose + view + size -> index canvas -> RGB.

index canvas: mat (int, -1 = empty) and step (0..5). The palette turns (mat, step) into colour.
size_px is the animal's own nose-to-tail-root length in pixels (a 60 px bear and a 60 px cougar
are both 60 px from nose to rump; the cougar's tail is extra). For true relative scale use
paint_k with pixels per metre.
"""
import os
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import replace
import rig, render, coat
from render import View

GROUND = 20
GSHADOW = 21
CONTACT = 22
MICRO = int(os.environ.get("MICRO", 12))
_HB = {}


def hb_m(ind):
    key = (ind.species, ind.sex, ind.age, ind.season, ind.seed, round(ind.months, 2))
    if key not in _HB:
        _HB[key] = rig.hb_length(ind)
    return _HB[key]


def contact_shadow(R, tubes, view, k):
    f = render.view_xf(view)
    tor = [t for t in tubes if t.name == "torso"][0]
    ox, oy = R.origin
    H, W = R.cov.shape
    c = tor.c
    n = len(c)
    u = np.linspace(0.1, 0.9, 24)
    idx = u * (n - 1)
    cx = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), c[:, 0])
    cy = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), c[:, 1])
    cz = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), c[:, 2] - tor.a)
    hw = np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), tor.b) * 0.95
    # only where the belly is near the ground (a rearing or climbing animal has no torso contact)
    near = cz < 0.9 * np.interp(idx, np.arange(n), tor.a) * 4
    pts = []
    for s in np.linspace(-1, 1, 9):
        pts.append(np.stack([cx[near], cy[near] + s * hw[near], np.zeros(near.sum())], 1))
    if not near.any():
        return R
    P = np.concatenate(pts)
    sx, sv, d = f(P)
    X = np.round(sx * k + ox).astype(int)
    Y = np.round(oy - sv * k).astype(int)
    m = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    ok = (X >= 0) & (X < W) & (Y >= 0) & (Y < H)
    m[Y[ok], X[ok]] = True
    from scipy import ndimage as ndi
    m = ndi.binary_closing(m, iterations=1) | m
    R.ground_shadow = np.maximum(R.ground_shadow, m.astype(float))
    return R


def far_parts(R, view, tubes):
    f = render.view_xf(view)
    tor = [t for t in tubes if t.name == "torso"][0]
    _, _, dmid = f(tor.c.mean(0))
    far = set()
    for i, t in enumerate(tubes):
        if t.name[:2] in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH") or t.name.startswith(("ear", "shoulder")):
            _, _, d = f(t.c.mean(0))
            if d > dmid + 0.02:
                far.add(i)
    return np.isin(R.part, list(far)) if far else np.zeros(R.part.shape, bool)


def value_field(R, view, tubes, ind):
    v = R.lit
    n = R.nrm
    sh = R.shadow > 0.5
    far = far_parts(R, view, tubes)
    if view.diffuse >= 0.5:
        x = 1.6 + 2.2 * np.clip(0.5 + 0.5 * n[..., 2], 0, 1) + 0.3 * np.clip(-n[..., 1], 0, 1)
        x[far] -= 1.0
        return np.clip(x, 0, 5), far
    x = np.zeros(v.shape)
    litf = (v > 0.04) & ~sh
    x[litf] = 2.8 + 2.35 * np.clip((v[litf] - 0.04) / 0.9, 0, 1)
    shf = ~litf
    x[shf] = 1.1
    core = shf & ~sh & (v > -0.3)
    x[core] = 0.45
    bounce = np.clip(-n[..., 2] - 0.2, 0, 1)
    x[shf] += 1.0 * bounce[shf]
    x[far] -= 1.1
    sh_parts = [i for i, t in enumerate(tubes) if t.name.startswith("shoulder")]
    sb = np.isin(R.part, sh_parts)
    x[sb] = np.maximum(x[sb], np.minimum(3.0, x[sb] + 1.6))
    if ind.species == "bear":
        # black fur: the lit family is a sheen, not a gradient. The back and the top of the head
        # catch the sky; everything else sits low. Compress the lit family toward its top and the
        # rest toward the shadow so the bear stays black and its form reads as a sheen line.
        up = np.clip(n[..., 2], 0, 1)
        x[litf] = 1.9 + 3.1 * np.clip((v[litf] - 0.3) / 0.65, 0, 1) ** 1.3 + 0.4 * up[litf]
    return np.clip(x, 0, 5), far


def paint_index(ind, gait="stand", phase=0.0, view=View(), size_px=60, pose=None, lines=True, k=None):
    sp = ind.spec()
    if pose is None:
        pose = rig.gait_pose(sp, gait, phase)
    tubes, sp = rig.build(ind, pose)
    if k is None:
        k = size_px / hb_m(ind)
    else:
        size_px = k * hb_m(ind)
    if size_px < MICRO:
        import micro
        R = micro.paint(ind, tubes, sp, view, k, size_px, pose)
        R.k = k
        R = contact_shadow(R, tubes, view, k)
        return R, tubes, k
    view = replace(view, ss=int(np.clip(round(300 / size_px), 3, 12)))
    R = render.rasterize(tubes, ind, view, k, pad=3)
    if lines:
        R = render.thin_lines(R, tubes, ind, view, k)
    on = (R.cov >= 0.45) | R.thin
    R.mat = np.where(on, R.mat, -1)
    x, far = value_field(R, view, tubes, ind)
    R.x, R.far, R.k = x, far, k
    R = contact_shadow(R, tubes, view, k)
    import hide, fringe
    dark = [rig.DARK, rig.LEG] + ([rig.BODY, rig.MUZZLE] if ind.species == "bear" else [])
    R = hide.apply(R, view, tubes, size_px, flatten=0.15, dark_mats=dark, species=ind.species)
    if ind.species == "bear" and size_px >= 24:
        R = fringe.shag(R, size_px, ind.seed, amount=sp.fur)
    if ind.species == "cougar" and size_px >= 30:
        R = fringe.interlock(R, rig.BODY, rig.BELLY, size_px, ind.seed + 9)
    return R, tubes, k


GREY_BASE = {
    rig.BODY: 0.56, rig.DARK: 0.26, rig.PALE: 0.84, rig.BELLY: 0.74, rig.LEG: 0.3, rig.CLAW: 0.2,
    rig.SPOT: 0.2, rig.TIP: 0.8, rig.NOSE: 0.1, rig.EARIN: 0.62, rig.THROAT: 0.6, rig.TAILTIP: 0.1,
    rig.MUZZLE: 0.5, rig.BLAZE: 0.9, rig.EYE: 0.05, rig.FACE: 0.42,
}
STEP_F = np.array([0.46, 0.56, 0.68, 0.80, 1.0, 1.16])


def grey(R, bg=0.62, flat=False, ind=None):
    H, W = R.mat.shape
    img = np.full((H, W), bg, float)
    img = np.where(R.ground_shadow > 0.4, bg * 0.62, img)
    on = R.mat >= 0
    gb = dict(GREY_BASE)
    if ind is not None and ind.species == "bear":
        gb[rig.BODY] = 0.2
    base = np.vectorize(lambda m: gb.get(m, 0.5))(np.where(on, R.mat, 0))
    f = STEP_F[R.step] if not flat else 1.0
    img = np.where(on, np.clip(base * f, 0, 1), img)
    return (img * 255).astype(np.uint8)


def notan(R, bg=230):
    img = np.full(R.mat.shape, bg, np.uint8)
    img[R.mat >= 0] = 30
    return img


def colour(R, ind, hour="morning", ground="summer", depth=0.0, bg=None, rng=None, gtype="meadow"):
    import palette as P
    tab = P.palette(ind, hour, depth, ground)
    H, W = R.mat.shape
    rng = rng or np.random.default_rng(0)
    if bg is None:
        import ground as G
        k = getattr(R, "k", 20.0)
        g = G.meadow((H, W), k, seed=int(rng.integers(1 << 30)))
        img = tab[P.GROUND][g]
        gs = R.ground_shadow > 0.45
        img[gs] = tab[P.GSHADOW][np.clip(g[gs], 0, 5)]
    else:
        img = bg.copy()
    on = R.mat >= 0
    img[on] = tab[R.mat[on], R.step[on]]
    return img

def shag(R, size_px, seed=0, amount=1.0):
    """The bear's coat breaks its silhouette: long guard hairs hang in 1-2 px ticks under the belly,
    the neck and the backs of the legs, and stand as occasional 1-px tufts along the back and
    rump. Deterministic in (seed, pixel)."""
    if amount <= 0:
        return R
    m = R.mat
    H, W = m.shape
    on = m >= 0
    out_m, out_s = m.copy(), R.step.copy()
    # hanging ticks: pixels whose lower neighbour is empty
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(on[:-1] & ~on[1:])
    L = 1 if size_px < 45 else 2
    for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
        hv = _h(y, x, seed + 31)
        if hv > 0.42 * amount:
            continue
        n = 1 + (hv < 0.14 * amount and L > 1)
        for j in range(1, n + 1):
            if y + j < H and not on[y + j, x]:
                out_m[y + j, x] = m[y, x]
                out_s[y + j, x] = max(0, R.step[y, x] - 1)
    # tufts on top: pixels whose upper neighbour is empty
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(on[1:] & ~on[:-1])
    for y, x in zip(ys + 1, xs):
        if _h(y, x, seed + 57) < 0.16 * amount and y - 1 >= 0:
            out_m[y - 1, x] = m[y, x]
            out_s[y - 1, x] = R.step[y, x]
    R.mat, R.step = out_m, out_s
    return R
'''

        except Exception as e: print("retry",e,file=sys.stderr,flush=True); time.sleep(20*(t+1))
    return {}
def members(c,typ):
    out=[];cont={}
    while True:
        d=api(action="query",list="categorymembers",cmtitle=c,cmlimit=500,cmtype=typ,**cont); time.sleep(2.5)
        out+=[m["title"] for m in d.get("query",{}).get("categorymembers",[])]
        if "continue" in d: cont={"cmcontinue":d["continue"]["cmcontinue"]}
        else: return out

"""The comparison board: photo (or Seton's drawing from life) | mine, at the same shoulder height,
on the same ground line; in notan (black silhouette), grey or colour; then the size rows, both
area-reduced to the sizes the animal is actually seen at.

    python3 board.py notan|grey|colour [tag]
    python3 board.py sizes [tag]
"""
import os, sys
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
import rig, paint
from render import View

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
REF = os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'refs') + '/'
NAT = '~/work/naturalist/animals/photos/'
OUT = os.path.join(HERE, '..', 'stages', 's1')

BOARD = [
    # file, crop, ground row, withers row, (species, sex), gait, phase, yaw, label
    (NAT + 'black_bear/openverse_Black_bears_in_Whistler_Olympic_Park_af309806.jpg', (320, 240, 820, 560), 510, 290,
     ('bear', 'm'), 'walk', 0.12, 12, 'black bear walking, Whistler (GoToVan, CC BY)'),
    (REF + 'board/seton_cougar_standing_plXII.png', (40, 100, 1060, 700), 600, 228,
     ('cougar', 'f'), 'stand', 0.0, 0, 'cougar standing, Seton pl. XII, from life'),
    (REF + 'board/seton_cougar_walking_plXIII.png', (20, 20, 720, 340), 325, 80,
     ('cougar', 'f'), 'walk', 0.55, 180, 'cougar walking, Seton pl. XIII, 1886'),
]
HPX = 150
EXTRA = []   # appended by later stages


def entries():
    return BOARD + EXTRA


def render_mine(spn, sex, g, ph, yaw, Hpx, mode, elev=0, season='summer'):
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, 'adult', season, 1)
    sp = ind.spec()
    k = Hpx / sp.H
    R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=elev, sun_el=45, sun_az=-145, ss=3), k=k)
    if mode == 'notan':
        return np.stack([paint.notan(R)] * 3, -1), R
    if mode == 'grey':
        return np.stack([paint.grey(R, bg=0.92, ind=ind)] * 3, -1), R
    return paint.colour(R, ind, 'morning', rng=np.random.default_rng(3)), R


def panel(e, mode):
    f, crop, gr, wr, (spn, sex), g, ph, yaw, lab = e
    im = Image.open(f).convert('RGB').crop(crop)
    s = HPX / (gr - wr)
    im = im.resize((int(im.width * s), int(im.height * s)), Image.LANCZOS)
    grow = int((gr - crop[1]) * s)
    mine, R = render_mine(spn, sex, g, ph, yaw, HPX, mode)
    H = max(im.height, mine.shape[0] + max(0, grow - R.ground_row)) + 4
    out = np.full((H, im.width + mine.shape[1] + 12, 3), 255, np.uint8)
    out[:im.height, :im.width] = np.array(im)
    y0 = grow - R.ground_row
    ys, ye = max(0, y0), min(H, y0 + mine.shape[0])
    out[ys:ye, im.width + 12:im.width + 12 + mine.shape[1]] = mine[ys - y0:ye - y0]
    # the shared ground line and withers line, faint
    for yy, col in ((grow, (220, 60, 60)), (grow - HPX, (60, 120, 220))):
        if 0 <= yy < H:
            out[yy, ::3] = col
    return out


def stack(ps, pad=6):
    W = max(p.shape[1] for p in ps)
    out = np.full((sum(p.shape[0] + pad for p in ps), W, 3), 255, np.uint8)
    y = 0
    for p in ps:
        out[y:y + p.shape[0], :p.shape[1]] = p
        y += p.shape[0] + pad
    return out


def sizes(e, Ls=(60, 30, 16, 8)):
    """Photo area-reduced to L px nose-to-rump | mine at L px."""
    f, crop, gr, wr, (spn, sex), g, ph, yaw, lab = e
    im = Image.open(f).convert('RGB').crop(crop)
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, 'adult', 'summer', 1)
    sp = ind.spec()
    hbH = paint.hb_m(ind) / sp.H              # nose-to-rump in shoulder heights, from the rig
    cells = []
    for L in Ls:
        s = (L / hbH) / (gr - wr)
        p = im.resize((max(1, int(im.width * s)), max(1, int(im.height * s))), Image.BOX)
        R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=4, sun_el=45, sun_az=-145), size_px=L)
        cells.append((np.array(p), paint.colour(R, ind, 'morning', rng=np.random.default_rng(2))))
    hh = max(max(a.shape[0], b.shape[0]) for a, b in cells)
    ww = sum(a.shape[1] + b.shape[1] + 6 for a, b in cells)
    t = np.full((hh, ww, 3), 255, np.uint8)
    x = 0
    for a, b in cells:
        t[hh - a.shape[0]:, x:x + a.shape[1]] = a; x += a.shape[1] + 2
        t[hh - b.shape[0]:, x:x + b.shape[1]] = b; x += b.shape[1] + 4
    return t


if __name__ == '__main__':
    mode = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'notan'
    tag = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 'a'
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    if mode == 'sizes':
        out = stack([sizes(e) for e in entries()], 4)
        Image.fromarray(out).resize((out.shape[1] * 4, out.shape[0] * 4), Image.NEAREST).save(f'{OUT}/sizes_{tag}.png')
    else:
        out = stack([panel(e, mode) for e in entries()])
        Image.fromarray(out).save(f'{OUT}/board_{mode}_{tag}.png')
    print('ok')

Bash: Download curated cougar Commons files slowly
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/bear-cougar/refs; cat > dl.py <<'EOF'
"""Slow downloader for Commons files: titles on stdin (one per line) -> <outdir>/<safe>.jpg at 960 px,
plus sources.tsv. 1 file per 3 s; imageinfo in batches of 40."""
import json,urllib.request,urllib.parse,time,sys,os,re
UA="pixelart-studies-refs/1.0 (research; bear-cougar; slow)"
out=sys.argv[1]; os.makedirs(out,exist_ok=True)
titles=[l.strip() for l in sys.stdin if l.strip().startswith("File:") and not l.strip().lower().endswith((".webm",".ogv",".pdf",".tif",".tiff"))]
def get(u,tries=5):
    for t in range(tries):
        try: return urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(u,headers={"User-Agent":UA}),timeout=60).read()
        except Exception as e:
            print("retry",e,file=sys.stderr,flush=True); time.sleep(15*(t+1))
    return None
src=open(os.path.join(out,"sources.tsv"),"a")
for i in range(0,len(titles),40):
    batch=titles[i:i+40]
    q=urllib.parse.urlencode({"action":"query","titles":"|".join(batch),"prop":"imageinfo","iiprop":"url|extmetadata","iiurlwidth":960,"format":"json"})
    d=get("https://commons.wikimedia.org/w/api.php?"+q)
    if not d: continue
    for p in json.loads(d)["query"]["pages"].values():
        ii=p.get("imageinfo",[{}])[0]; u=ii.get("thumburl")
        if not u: continue
        safe=re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]+","_",p["title"][5:])[:70]+".jpg"
        fn=os.path.join(out,safe)
        if os.path.exists(fn): continue
        b=get(u)
        if b:
            open(fn,"wb").write(b)
            m=ii.get("extmetadata",{}); strip=lambda s: re.sub("<[^>]+>","",s or "").strip()
            src.write("\t".join([safe,p["title"],strip(m.get("LicenseShortName",{}).get("value")),strip(m.get("Artist",{}).get("value"))[:80],ii.get("descriptionurl","")])+"\n"); src.flush()
            print("ok",safe,flush=True)
        time.sleep(3)
EOF

I found the research paper you were looking for! Here are the key findings from the Williams et al. 2014 Science study:

## Study Overview

The researchers developed and deployed a physiological SMART (species movement, acceleration, and radio tracking) collar that used accelerometry to continuously monitor energetics, movements, and behavior of free-ranging pumas.

## Main Results

Pumas displayed marked individuality in predatory activities, ranging from low-cost sit-and-wait behaviors to constant movements with energetic costs averaging 2.3 times those predicted for running mammals.

Pumas reduce these costs by remaining cryptic and precisely matching maximum pouncing force (overall dynamic body acceleration = 5.3 to 16.1g) to prey size.

## Key Insight

Such instantaneous energetics help to explain why most felids stalk and pounce, and their analysis represents a powerful approach for accurately forecasting resource demands required for survival by large, mobile predators.

The study was published in Science, Volume 346, Issue 6205, pages 81-85 (DOI: 10.1126/science.1254885). The research was conducted by scientists at UC Santa Cruz and used novel tracking technology to provide unprecedented insights into puma hunting behavior and energy expenditure in the wild.

"""Contact sheet of a folder of photos with index numbers: python3 contact.py DIR OUT [cols] [thumb]"""
import sys, glob, os
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
d, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
cols = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 6
tw = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 240
fs = sorted(f for f in glob.glob(d + '/*') if f.lower().endswith(('.jpg', '.png', '.jpeg')) and not os.path.basename(f).startswith('_'))
th = int(tw * 0.75)
rows = (len(fs) + cols - 1) // cols
sheet = Image.new('RGB', (cols * (tw + 4), rows * (th + 16)), (20, 20, 24))
dr = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
for i, f in enumerate(fs):
    try:
        im = Image.open(f).convert('RGB'); im.thumbnail((tw, th))
    except Exception:
        continue
    x, y = (i % cols) * (tw + 4), (i // cols) * (th + 16)
    sheet.paste(im, (x, y + 14)); dr.text((x + 2, y), f"{i} {os.path.basename(f)[:34]}", fill=(220, 220, 220))
sheet.save(out); print(len(fs), sheet.size)
EOF
echo ok

Write: elements/bear-cougar/src/micro.py
"""The small painter (under ~12 px nose-to-rump): a designed glyph placed from the rig's keypoints.

Below about a dozen pixels the splat renderer averages a 3-D body the way a camera does, and a
camera's 8 px bear is a smudge. A pixel artist at this size PLACES pixels: a body mass, legs,
a head, and the one or two marks that say which animal it is. The rules, per species:

BLACK BEAR  (the naturalist: "a black lump in a green meadow, head down"; Seton's own silhouette
            vignettes, pl. p.118, are this painter's model)
  body    a rounded mass per column, deep (~0.62 of the shoulder height in rows), the highest
          column over the hips, the rump rounded off, the front sloping into the neck
  head    lower than the back line: the neck and head run forward and DOWN from the shoulder;
          the last pixel of the muzzle is the brown MUZZLE colour (the warm mark that says which
          way the bear faces) from 6 px up
  ears    two round ears: a tick above the head at >= 8 px (the bear's ears are prominent)
  legs    short and thick: the near pair as clean runs, a 2nd px column at >= 10 px; the far pair
          one step darker, only when apart
  tail    none

COUGAR      (the naturalist: "long, low, the tail visible"; "a long pale shape ... tail swinging")
  body    long and shallow (~0.46 of the shoulder height in rows) on long legs; the croup a row
          higher than the withers at >= 9 px
  underside  the bottom row of the body in the pale BELLY colour when the body has >= 3 rows
  head    small: 1-2 px ahead of and a little below the withers line, an ear tick at >= 9 px
  tail    THE mark: a 1-px run from the rump, falling back and curling up at the tip, with the
          tip pixel black (TAILTIP); it is drawn at every size down to 4 px
  legs    long, 1 px; far legs darker, only when apart

Small figures against a low sun: dark silhouettes with the sun's colour on the top edge, broken
(from the elk study).
"""
import numpy as np
import dataclasses
import rig, render
from render import clean_line, view_xf


def _put(mat, step, part, x, y, m, s, p, overwrite=True):
    H, W = mat.shape
    x, y = int(round(x)), int(round(y))
    if 0 <= x < W and 0 <= y < H and (overwrite or mat[y, x] < 0):
        mat[y, x] = m
        step[y, x] = s
        part[y, x] = p


def _line(mat, step, part, a, b, m, s, p, overwrite=True):
    for (x, y) in clean_line(a[0], a[1], b[0], b[1]):
        _put(mat, step, part, x, y, m, s, p, overwrite)


def paint(ind, tubes, sp, view, k, size_px, pose=None):
    f = view_xf(view)
    names = [t.name for t in tubes]
    Rall = render.rasterize(tubes, ind, dataclasses.replace(view, ss=8), k, pad=4)
    H, W = Rall.cov.shape
    ox, oy = Rall.origin
    mat = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)
    step = np.zeros((H, W), np.int32)
    part = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int32)
    bear = ind.species == "bear"

    def P(p):
        sx, sv, d = f(np.asarray(p))
        return np.stack([sx * k + ox, oy - sv * k], -1)

    def depth(p):
        return f(np.asarray(p))[2]

    ti = names.index("torso")
    tor = tubes[ti]
    lying = pose is not None and (pose.lie > 0.5)
    upright = pose is not None and abs(pose.pitch) > 35
    # --- body silhouette per column from projected surface rings
    th = np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 24, endpoint=False)
    T, N1, N2 = render._frames(tor.c, tor.up)
    ring = tor.c[:, None, :] + tor.a[:, None, None] * np.cos(th)[None, :, None] * N1[:, None, :] + tor.b[:, None, None] * np.sin(th)[None, :, None] * N2[:, None, :]
    pr = P(ring.reshape(-1, 3))
    xs, ys = pr[:, 0], pr[:, 1]
    front_right = P(tor.c[0])[0] > P(tor.c[-1])[0]    # torso stations run front -> rear
    hpx = sp.H * k                                      # shoulder height in px
    x0, x1 = int(np.floor(xs.min() + 0.5)), int(np.floor(xs.max() - 0.5))
    cols = {}
    for cx in range(x0, max(x1, x0 + 1) + 1):
        sel = np.abs(xs - (cx + 0.5)) < 0.75
        if not sel.any():
            continue
        top, bot = ys[sel].min(), ys[sel].max()
        cols[cx] = (int(round(top - 0.1)), int(round(bot + 0.4)))
    if not upright and not lying:
        # the far read is a mass of the right depth: bears deep, cougars shallow
        target = max(1, int(round((0.62 if bear else 0.44) * hpx)))
        for cx, (t, b) in list(cols.items()):
            if b - t < target:
                cols[cx] = (b - target, b)
    cks = sorted(cols)
    span = cks[-1] - cks[0] + 1

    def ufront(cx):   # 0 at the rear, 1 at the front
        u = (cx - cks[0]) / max(span - 1, 1)
        return u if front_right else 1 - u

    if not upright and not lying and len(cks) >= 4:
        for cx in cks:
            t, b = cols[cx]
            u = ufront(cx)
            if bear:
                # rump highest (u ~ 0.2-0.35), rounded off at both ends
                if (u < 0.08 or u > 0.92) and b - t > 2:
                    t += 1
                if 0.15 < u < 0.4 and size_px >= 9:
                    t -= 0 if b - t >= 4 else 0
            else:
                if u < 0.3 and size_px >= 9:
                    t -= 1                                  # the high croup
                if (u < 0.06 or u > 0.94) and b - t > 1:
                    t += 1
            cols[cx] = (t, b)
    base_mat = rig.BODY
    for cx in cks:
        t, b = cols[cx]
        for y in range(t, b):
            m = base_mat
            s = 3 if y == t else 2
            if bear:
                s = 2 if y == t else 1
            elif y == b - 1 and b - t >= 3:
                m = rig.BELLY
            _put(mat, step, part, cx, y, m, s, ti)

    def belly_y(x):
        x = int(round(x))
        near = min(cols, key=lambda c: abs(c - x))
        return cols[near][1] - 1

    # --- legs
    pairs = {"F": [], "H": []}
    for L in ("LF", "RF", "LH", "RH"):
        segs = [tubes[names.index(L + s)] for s in ("1", "2", "3", "pw")]
        chain = np.array([segs[0].c[1], segs[0].c[-1], segs[1].c[-1], segs[2].c[-1], segs[3].c[-1]])
        pairs[L[1]].append((depth(chain.mean(0)), L, P(chain)))
    for key in ("F", "H"):
        pr_ = sorted(pairs[key], key=lambda t: t[0])
        near, far = pr_[0], pr_[1]
        drawn = [near]
        if abs(near[2][-2][0] - far[2][-2][0]) >= 1.6 and size_px >= 5:
            drawn.append(far)
        for d, L, pts in drawn[::-1]:
            is_far = (L == far[1]) and len(drawn) == 2
            p0 = pts[1].copy() if not (upright or lying) else pts[1].copy()
            if not (upright or lying):
                p0[1] = min(p0[1], belly_y(p0[0]))
            chain = [p0] + ([pts[2]] if size_px >= 10 else []) + [pts[3]]
            s = 0 if is_far else 1
            m = rig.BODY
            for a, b in zip(chain[:-1], chain[1:]):
                _line(mat, step, part, a, b, m, s, names.index(L + "2"), overwrite=False)
                if bear and size_px >= 10 and not is_far:
                    off = np.array([-1.0 if front_right else 1.0, 0]) if key == "F" else np.array([1.0 if front_right else -1.0, 0])
                    _line(mat, step, part, a + off, b + off, m, s, names.index(L + "2"), overwrite=False)
    # --- neck and head
    neck = tubes[names.index("neck")]
    head = tubes[names.index("head")]
    nb = P(neck.c[1])
    poll = P(neck.c[-1])
    nose = P(head.c[-1])
    hm = rig.BODY
    hs = 2 if bear else 3
    _line(mat, step, part, nb, poll, hm, hs - (1 if bear else 0), names.index("neck"), overwrite=False)
    if bear and size_px >= 7:
        # a thick neck: doubled below
        _line(mat, step, part, nb + [0, 1], poll + [0, 1], hm, 1, names.index("neck"), overwrite=False)
    if size_px >= 6:
        _line(mat, step, part, poll, nose, hm, hs, names.index("head"))
        if size_px >= 9:
            mid = poll * 0.6 + nose * 0.4
            _line(mat, step, part, poll + [0, 1], mid + [0, 1], hm, max(hs - 1, 1), names.index("head"), overwrite=False)
    else:
        _put(mat, step, part, nose[0], nose[1], hm, hs, names.index("head"), overwrite=False)
    if bear and size_px >= 6:
        _put(mat, step, part, nose[0], nose[1], rig.MUZZLE, 3, names.index("head"))
    if not bear and size_px >= 10:
        _put(mat, step, part, nose[0], nose[1] + 0.6, rig.PALE, 4, names.index("head"))
    # --- ears
    ears = sorted([t for t in tubes if t.name.startswith("ear")], key=lambda t: depth(t.c.mean(0)))
    if size_px >= (8 if bear else 9):
        e = ears[0]
        pe = P(e.c[-1])
        _put(mat, step, part, pe[0], pe[1], rig.BODY if bear else rig.DARK, 2, names.index(e.name), overwrite=False)
        if bear and size_px >= 11:
            pe2 = P(ears[1].c[-1])
            if abs(pe2[0] - pe[0]) >= 1:
                _put(mat, step, part, pe2[0], pe2[1], rig.BODY, 1, names.index(ears[1].name), overwrite=False)
    # --- the cougar's tail: the giveaway
    if not bear and size_px >= 4:
        tail = tubes[names.index("tail")]
        pts = P(tail.c)
        idx = np.linspace(0, len(pts) - 1, max(3, int(size_px / 3))).astype(int)
        pp = pts[idx]
        for a, b in zip(pp[:-1], pp[1:]):
            _line(mat, step, part, a, b, rig.BODY, 2, names.index("tail"), overwrite=False)
        _put(mat, step, part, pp[-1][0], pp[-1][1], rig.TAILTIP, 1, names.index("tail"))
    # --- a carried salmon: one silver pixel either side of the jaws
    if pose is not None and pose.carry == "salmon" and size_px >= 6:
        s_ = tubes[names.index("salmon")]
        for q in P(s_.c[[0, -1]]):
            _put(mat, step, part, q[0], q[1], rig.TIP, 4, names.index("salmon"))
    # --- back light
    Lw = render.sun_vec(view.sun_az, view.sun_el)
    if Lw[1] > 0.4 and view.diffuse < 0.5:
        on_ = mat >= 0
        step[on_] = np.minimum(step[on_], 1)
        top_ = on_ & ~np.vstack([np.zeros((1, W), bool), on_[:-1]])
        brk = ((np.arange(W)[None, :] * 73 + np.arange(H)[:, None] * 151) % 7) == 0
        rimp = top_ & ~brk
        mat[rimp] = 19
        step[rimp] = 4
    Rall.mat, Rall.step, Rall.part = mat, step, part
    Rall.cov = (mat >= 0).astype(float)
    Rall.x = step.astype(float)
    return Rall

Bash: Fix cougar leg geometry
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/bear-cougar/src; python3 - <<'EOF'

Write: elements/bear-cougar/src/ladder.py
"""Size ladder: subjects x sizes (nose-to-rump px), landscape and iso.   python3 ladder.py TAG [sizes]"""
import os, sys
os.environ.setdefault("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "1")
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from multiprocessing import Pool
import rig, paint
from render import View

SUBJ = [("bear", "m", "adult", "walk", 0.2), ("bear", "f", "adult", "graze", 0.0), ("cougar", "f", "adult", "walk", 0.3),
        ("cougar", "m", "adult", "stalk", 0.3), ("cougar", "f", "adult", "stand", 0.0)]
SIZES = [60, 44, 32, 24, 18, 14, 12, 10, 8, 6, 5, 4]


def cell(a):
    spn, sex, age, g, ph, L, yaw, elev = a
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, sex, age, "summer", 3)
    R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=yaw, elev=elev, sun_az=-150, sun_el=35), size_px=L)
    return paint.colour(R, ind, "morning", rng=np.random.default_rng(1))


def build(tag, sizes=SIZES, subj=SUBJ, Z=4):
    jobs, rows = [], []
    for view in ((-12, 6), (-35, 30)):
        for s in subj:
            rows.append((s, view))
            for L in sizes:
                jobs.append(s + (L,) + view)
    with Pool(4) as p:
        tiles = p.map(cell, jobs)
    n = len(sizes)
    tiles = [tiles[i * n:(i + 1) * n] for i in range(len(rows))]
    cw = [max(tiles[r][c].shape[1] for r in range(len(rows))) for c in range(n)]
    rh = [max(t.shape[0] for t in row) for row in tiles]
    W = sum(cw) + 4 * n
    Hh = sum(rh) + 4 * len(rows)
    out = np.full((Hh, W, 3), 24, np.uint8)
    y = 0
    for r, row in enumerate(tiles):
        x = 0
        for c, t in enumerate(row):
            out[y + rh[r] - t.shape[0]:y + rh[r], x:x + t.shape[1]] = t
            x += cw[c] + 4
        y += rh[r] + 4
    os.makedirs("../stages/s2", exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f"../stages/s2/ladder_{tag}.png")
    print("ok", out.shape)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "a"
    sizes = [int(v) for v in sys.argv[2].split(",")] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else SIZES
    build(tag, sizes)

# Notebook: black bear and cougar

Raw working thread, in order. Images referenced by path under `stages/`.

## 1. Reading (13:00-13:25)

- Read the elk post, tutorial and all seven how-tos; the elk code (rig, render, paint, hide, fringe, palette, micro, sheets).
- Naturalist: `predators.md`, `drawing-animals.md`. The four things for these two:
  - bear: rump higher than shoulders, straight face, prominent ears, short thick legs, round barrel; "a black lump in a green meadow, head down".
  - cougar: long, low, heavy-hindquartered, small round head, tail half the body, carried low with an up-curl; stalking head below the shoulder blades.
- Photo sheets: `black_bear` is good (Whistler walker, Olympic male on rocks, grazing bears); `cougar` is poor (zoo, taxidermy).
- Muybridge catalogue (UPenn PDF): no bear. Felids: 716-720 cat (walk -> gallop, trot, gallop), 721-728 lion, 729-730 tigress, 731 jaguar; 744-745 raccoon (the only plantigrade). Commons 429s the plates (shared IP, rate-limited); logged, moved on.
- Bear gaits: Shine et al. 2015 JEB (grizzly): walk 1.1-2.0 m/s, running walk 2-3, canter > 3 m/s; NO trot; duty(walk) = 0.76 - 0.12 v; forefoot placed medially, carpus rotates medially in swing, "waddle". SICB abstract (black bears): transverse walk slow, a PACE at intermediate speeds, lower lateral forces than grizzlies.

## 2. The model system: Seton (13:10-13:35)

Found by searching the naturalist-painter tradition: Ernest Thompson Seton, *Studies in the Art Anatomy of Animals* (1896, archive.org cihm_35627) and *Lives of Game Animals* vol I (1925, cats) and II (1926, bears), all on archive.org, all public domain.

- Art Anatomy is a head-unit canon, like figure drawing: "The Grizzly, Brown and Black Bears go in a square of two heads ... the arch of the back rises considerably above the line"; neck 3/4 head; width at the shoulders one head. "A Cat goes in a rectangle of 2 1/2 heads in height by 3 1/2 heads in length" and the cougar "in all its proportions and anatomy agrees with the Cat". Tail = half the total length. "Whatever be the figure that includes the animal's body, the same figure of but half the dimensions usually appears in the space comprised by the belly, legs and ground."
- Plate XXVII, the proportions of the lion (refs/lit/seton_plates/seton_lion_proportions_plXXVII.png): scapula, humerus, radius, femur, tibia each one head; metatarsus 3/4; paw 1/5; belly at 1 1/4 of 2 1/2 heads.
- Plate XXVI, the lion skeleton; Plate XXV, the cat's muscles.
- Lives of Game Animals: size tables (black bear sow, Colorado: snout-tail 1613 mm, tail 127, hind foot 184, shoulder 648 mm; cougar male total 2287 mm, female 1982 mm with tail 814 mm), colour ("deep glossy black everywhere, excepting the muzzle, which is more or less brown, and the white patch commonly seen on the breast"; cougar "pale yellowish sandy brown ... lips white, with a dark spot at the base of the whiskers; the back of the ears dark ... the tip of the tail nearly black"; F. c. olympus of the Olympic Mountains "very dark in colour"; young spotted till ~6 months).
- Seton's drawings from life: pl. XVII blackbear poses (sitting like a person, rearing, climbing, on a rock); the notan vignette on p.118 (standing, sitting and sprawled bears as black silhouettes: the small-figure model); cougar pl. X, XII (standing, side view, from life: used on the board), XIII (walking), XV (pen sketches: crouch, lying, stalking).
- Climbing (Seton, bear): "more like the action of a Cat ... went up twice as fast as any man"; comes down tail first; "circles around the body of the tree with a series of leaps, ascending corkscrew fashion".

## 3. First rig (13:25-13:35)

Forked the elk's renderer, hide rules, fringe and palette machinery; wrote a new rig (plantigrade/digitigrade legs: upper bone -> IK joint -> lower bone -> metapodial at a pose angle -> digits), coat regions, palette.

- stages/s1/t1_grey.png: the first render. The bear already reads as a bear; the cougar has stilt legs, a horse's head, and a tail that pools on the ground.

## 4. The board (13:38-)

board.py: photo | mine at 150 px shoulder, same ground row. References: the Whistler walker (bear), Seton pl. XII (cougar standing, from life), pl. XIII (walking), later ODFW's walking cougar (Commons, camera ~25 deg up).

- b1 (notan): bear head too long and horizontal, spike ears; cougar legs short and oddly bent, head a horse's, tail too thick and pooled.
- b2: cougar torso raised (sternum 0.5 H, Seton canon), croup raised 0.08 H above the withers (pl. XII shows ~0.1 H), tail thinner and swung back, head shortened to 0.31 H; bear belly lowered (the Whistler bear's belly is ~0.3-0.35 H off the ground).
- b3/b4: the bear's walking head was still horizontal because the WALK table overrode the standing carriage: neck -12, head -42 -> the nose at ~0.42 H, as in the photo.
- Cougar legs: printed the joints. Forearm was slanting forward because the paw was set ahead of the shoulder joint; a cougar's forearm is vertical under the elbow. Hind: femur 0.30, tibia 0.40, metatarsus 0.27 at 28 deg, paw under the hip: the hock at 0.26 H, behind.
- sizes_b5: at 60-8 px the bear is too grey (the sheen family lit too much and the hair dither mottled it). The photo bear is black with a sheen only on the back. Darker ramp, no dither on black fur except in the sheen.

# voxsim infrastructure: where work runs, where things live

For every lane. Read this once. Everything here is meant to be **easy and fast**. If any step takes more than about 10 minutes of fiddling, stop and say so in your NOTES and your report, and ask for help. Don't grind on infrastructure.

## Credentials

- `~/work/.env.local` holds Modal and AWS keys. Load them with `set -a; . ~/work/.env.local; set +a`.
- **Never print, echo, log or commit their values.**

## Where compute runs
| Work | Where |
|---|---|
| Reading, writing code, small checks, `cargo check` on one crate | Your worktree, locally. `.cargo/config.toml` caps builds at 2 jobs. |
| Release builds of the workspace, long sims or soaks, render sweeps, heavy numpy | **Modal** (below). The laptop crashed from memory pressure with 14 lanes building at once, so don't run more than one heavy local job at a time. |
| Self-contained work that only needs the repo | Cloud agents; the manager decides. |

## Modal

- **Workspace:** set `MODAL_ENVIRONMENT=main` after loading the env file (the local `~/.modal.toml` defaults to another workspace's environment). A probe container with 16 CPUs came up in about 6 s.
- **Image:** use `modal.Image.from_registry("rust:1-bookworm", add_python="3.12")`. It ships current Rust. `debian_slim().apt_install(...)` failed on a stale Debian security mirror, so don't use it for Rust.
- **Budget:** the whole project has about $30 of Modal credit for now.
  - Size containers to the job, and set `timeout=`.
  - Shut down what you start (no idle `keep_warm`).
  - Log each Modal run's resources and wall time in your NOTES, so spend can be tracked.
  - If a job will take more than ~1 CPU-hour, say so before you run it.
- **Volumes:** use them for joint disk-and-CPU work (a cargo target cache per lane, shared data). Name everything `voxsim-<lane>-<what>` (e.g. `voxsim-place-yakima-target`), so nothing sprawls untracked.
- **Tracking:** record every app, volume and S3 prefix you create in your lane's NOTES under "Remote resources".
- **Shared build helper: `tools/remote/vr`**. `vr build --release -p <crate>` and `vr run --out <dir> -- <cmd>` run your worktree (uncommitted edits included) on Modal. Each lane gets a cached target dir, and shared data is mounted at `/data`. A warm one-line rebuild of vox-app takes about 14 s and costs well under a cent. Two-minute guide: [tools/remote/README.md](../tools/remote/README.md).

## Storage

- **S3 bucket: live.** `civearth-files` in us-east-1 (`CIVEARTH_BUCKET_NAME`).
  - **Locally:** load the env file, then use `aws s3 cp/sync` or boto3.
  - **On Modal:** attach `secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name("voxsim-aws")]` (it sets the AWS keys, region and bucket name).
  - **Speed:** S3 is fast from Modal and slow from the laptop's home uplink (~2 MB/s), so fetch public data straight into S3 or a Modal volume from a container, not through the laptop.
  - **Prefix everything:**
    - `voxsim/data/<source>/…` for shared raw data (lidar, DEMs,

Bash: Print cougar glyph character map
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/bear-cougar/src; cat > glyphdbg.py <<'EOF'
import sys, numpy as np, rig, paint
from render import View
spn, g, L = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], float(sys.argv[3])
ind = rig.Individual(spn, "f", "adult", "summer", 3)
R, tubes, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, 0.3, View(yaw=-12, elev=6, sun_az=-150, sun_el=35), size_px=L)
ch = {-1: ".", 0: "B", 1: "D", 3: "b", 12: "M", 11: "T", 14: "e", 9: "i", 2: "p", 19: "R"}
names = [t.name for t in tubes]
rows = np.nonzero((R.mat >= 0).any(1))[0]; cols = np.nonzero((R.mat >= 0).any(0))[0]
for y in range(rows.min(), rows.max() + 1):

"""Pose check sheet: every pose and gait, grey or colour.   python3 poses.py TAG [grey|colour] [size] [elev]"""
import os, sys
os.environ.setdefault("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "1")
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from multiprocessing import Pool
import rig, paint
from render import View

BEAR = [("stand", 0), ("walk", 0.2), ("walk", 0.7), ("pace", 0.3), ("run", 0.4), ("run", 0.9), ("graze", 0), ("sniff", 0),
        ("sit", 0), ("stand_up", 0), ("lie", 0), ("fish", 0.2), ("fish", 0.7), ("climb", 0.0), ("climb", 0.5), ("carry", 0.3)]
COUG = [("stand", 0), ("alert", 0), ("walk", 0.2), ("walk", 0.7), ("stalk", 0.3), ("trot", 0.25), ("run", 0.4), ("run", 0.9),
        ("sit", 0), ("lie", 0), ("crouch", 0), ("pounce", 0.1), ("pounce", 0.5), ("pounce", 0.85)]


def cell(a):
    spn, g, ph, mode, L, elev = a
    ind = rig.Individual(spn, "m" if spn == "bear" else "f", "adult", "summer", 2)
    R, _, k = paint.paint_index(ind, g, ph, View(yaw=-10, elev=elev, sun_az=-150, sun_el=38), size_px=L)
    if mode == "grey":
        return np.stack([paint.grey(R, bg=0.9, ind=ind)] * 3, -1)
    return paint.colour(R, ind, "morning", rng=np.random.default_rng(4))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    tag = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "a"
    mode = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "grey"
    L = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 48
    elev = float(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 6
    jobs = [("bear", g, ph, mode, L, elev) for g, ph in BEAR] + [("cougar", g, ph, mode, L, elev) for g, ph in COUG]
    with Pool(4) as p:
        tiles = p.map(cell, jobs)
    rows = [tiles[:len(BEAR)], tiles[len(BEAR):]]
    Z = 3
    cw = max(t.shape[1] for t in tiles)
    rh = [max(t.shape[0] for t in r) for r in rows]
    ncol = 8
    lines = []
    for r in rows:
        for i in range(0, len(r), ncol):
            lines.append(r[i:i + ncol])
    H = sum(max(t.shape[0] for t in ln) + 6 for ln in lines)
    out = np.full((H, ncol * (cw + 6), 3), 30, np.uint8)
    y = 0
    for ln in lines:
        h = max(t.shape[0] for t in ln)
        for j, t in enumerate(ln):
            out[y + h - t.shape[0]:y + h, j * (cw + 6):j * (cw + 6) + t.shape[1]] = t
        y += h + 6
    os.makedirs("../stages/s2", exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray(out.repeat(Z, 0).repeat(Z, 1)).save(f"../stages/s2/poses_{tag}.png")
    print("ok")
